# ChestXray14_Multilabel_Classification

## 기본 정보
- **진료과:** Pulmonology
- **프로젝트:** ChestXray14_Multilabel_Classification
- **task_type:** classification
- **required_data:** [png, jpg, jpeg]
- **result_type:** [gradcam_overlay, classification_probabilities]
- **provides:** []
- **requires:** []

## 설명
Multi-label classifier for fourteen thoracic findings on a chest radiograph (TorchXRayVision). Input: chest X-ray (frontal or lateral radiograph); file formats png, jpg, jpeg. Assesses: atelectasis, cardiomegaly, effusion, infiltration, mass, nodule, pneumonia, pneumothorax, consolidation, edema, emphysema, fibrosis, pleural thickening, hernia. Outputs: per-finding probabilities, Grad-CAM overlay.
